Does a pinned piece's protection count for safety of a checking piece? In chess, a piece is considered protected if it could capture the opposing piece on the next move without leaving its own king in check. If the defender is absolutely pinned (king in check if it moves), it cannot capture. However, if the defender is pinned to the king, it cannot move at all unless it captures the checking piece?
